                                                  subtree_text: The security report is received and is assigned a primary handler. This person will coordinate the fix and release process. The problem is validated against all supported Node.js versions. Once confirmed, a list of all affected versions is determined. Code is audited to find any potential similar problems. Fixes are prepared for all supported releases. These fixes are not committed to the public repository but rather held locally pending the announcement.

                                                subtree_text: ServerResponse.writeEarlyHints() accepts a link header value that is set by the application. Passing arbitrary strings, including CRLF sequences, as the link value is an application-level misuse of the API, not a Node.js vulnerability.
